## 1. What happens under BANGBOMB when both “declares a Dodge” and “applies a MOD to their Dodge Rolls” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. The user declares a Dodge. The user applies a MOD to their Dodge Rolls. This MOD is listed in brackets after the name of this piece of Equipment in the user’s Unit Profile.

**Sources:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 120 — BANGBOMB

## 2. What happens under BIOMETRIC VISOR when both “ignores the WIP-3 MOD imposed by the Impersonation-1 State” and “If the user passes a Discover Roll against a Trooper in Impersonation-1 State” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. The user ignores the WIP-3 MOD imposed by the Impersonation-1 State. If the user passes a Discover Roll against a Trooper in Impersonation-1 State, that Trooper’s Impersonation State is canceled and their IMP-1 Marker is replaced with the corresponding Model.

**Sources:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 120 — BIOMETRIC VISOR

## 3. What happens under CAMOUFLAGED when both “Automatic during the Deployment Phase for Troopers with the Camouflage Skill” and “During the Active Turn, Troopers with the Camouflage Skill may only return to this…” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. Automatic during the Deployment Phase for Troopers with the Camouflage Skill. During the Active Turn, Troopers with the Camouflage Skill may only return to this state by spending a Long Skill, while outside the LoF of enemy Markers or Troopers.

**Sources:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 157 — CAMOUFLAGED

## 4. What happens under COORDINATED ORDER when both “Up to 4 Troopers can participate in a Coordinated Order” and “When players declare a Coordinated Order” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. Up to 4 Troopers can participate in a Coordinated Order. When players declare a Coordinated Order, they must name one of the participating Troopers as that Order’s Spearhead and place a Spearhead Token (SPEARHEAD) beside them.

**Sources:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 129 — COORDINATED ORDER

## 5. What happens under CUBE 2.0 when both “When using the Doctor Special Skill (or other Special Skills or pieces of Equipment…” and “applies a +2 MOD to their Saving Roll when making a Saving Roll against…” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. When using the Doctor Special Skill (or other Special Skills or pieces of Equipment that explicitly say so) to heal a Trooper with a Cube 2.0, you can expend Command Tokens to re-roll a failed WIP Roll. The user applies a +2 MOD to their Saving Roll when making a Saving Roll against an Attack with Sepsitor or Sepsitor Plus.

**Sources:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 121 — CUBE 2.0

## 10. What happens under JOURNALIST when both “within the Zone of Control of any allied Troopers with the Journalist Special Skill…” and “Similarly, Troopers within the Zone of Control of any enemy Troopers with the Journalist…” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. Troopers within the Zone of Control of any allied Troopers with the Journalist Special Skill apply a +3 MOD to their Guts Roll. Similarly, Troopers within the Zone of Control of any enemy Troopers with the Journalist Special Skill apply a -3 MOD to their Guts Roll.

**Sources:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 99 — JOURNALIST

## 47. What happens under SIXTH SENSE when both “Allows the user to respond to Attacks (and only Attacks) directed at them by…” and “If the user is the target of a BS Attack through a Zero Visibility…” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. Allows the user to respond to Attacks (and only Attacks) directed at them by enemies outside their LoF. For the purposes of drawing Line of Fire to the attackers, the user has a 360° LoF arc, and if they are in Engaged State, they can draw LoF to attackers outside their Close Combat. **Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. Directly inflict at least one Wound on an Enemy Trooper, or cause them to enter Dead State. When Frenzy Troopers gain the Impetuous Special Skill, they cancel any Marker State they are in (Camouflaged, Impersonation...), and any other States that say so. These Troopers cannot re-enter those States. Sibylla explains The Requirements for this Special Skill demand that the Frenzy Trooper must inflict the Wound directly on the target, or cause them to enter Dead State. The Trooper’s Deployable Weapons are not taken into account for the fulfillment of the Requirements.

## 77. What happens under REQUEST A SPECBALL when both “Have a Trooper in a non-Null state with the Spec-Ops Special Skill” and “When Requesting a Speedball during their Active Turn” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. Have a Trooper in a non-Null state with the Spec-Ops Special Skill. When Requesting a Speedball during their Active Turn, players must place one 55 mm SpecBall Token on the game table applying the Combat Jump Skill rules, and then make a PH Roll for it with a PH value of 15. ## 87. What happens under DOCTOR when both “using this Special Skill must be in Silhouette contact with their target” and “By passing a Normal WIP Roll, the target’s Unconscious State is cancelled, by removing…” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. Troopers using this Special Skill must be in Silhouette contact with their target. By passing a Normal WIP Roll, the target’s Unconscious State is cancelled, by removing 1 single Wound from those it has received, unless a rule or Skill specified otherwise. If the WIP Roll fails, the target automatically enters the Dead State and is removed from the game table.

**Sources:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 90 — DOCTOR

## 107. What happens under SIXTH SENSE when both “Allows the user to respond to Attacks (and only Attacks) directed at them by…” and “If the user declares Dodge or Reset” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. Allows the user to respond to Attacks (and only Attacks) directed at them by enemies outside their LoF. For the purposes of drawing Line of Fire to the attackers, the user has a 360° LoF arc, and if they are in Engaged State, they can draw LoF to attackers outside their Close Combat. If the user declares Dodge or Reset, no negative MODs are applied, with the exception of the -6 PH MOD for Immobilized-A State, the -3 WIP MOD for Immobilized-B State, and the -9 WIP MOD for Isolated State.

**Sources:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 111 — SIXTH SENSE

## 125. What happens under BIOMETRIC VISOR when both “If the user passes a Discover Roll against a Trooper in Impersonation-1 State” and “Users of a Biometric Visor ignore Surprise Attack MODs from attackers benefitting from Impersonation…” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. If the user passes a Discover Roll against a Trooper in Impersonation-1 State, that Trooper’s Impersonation State is canceled and their IMP-1 Marker is replaced with the corresponding Model. Users of a Biometric Visor ignore Surprise Attack MODs from attackers benefitting from Impersonation or Holoecho States, if they have LoF to the attacker. In addition, they also ignore Surprise Attack MODs from CC Attacks from those attackers even if they don’t have LoF to the attacker.

## 140. What happens under ALERT! when both “At the end of the Order, during its Conclusion, and after performing any Saving…” and “This movement is automatic, it does not require a Roll and does not generate…” apply?

At the end of the Order, during its Conclusion, and after performing any Saving Rolls, Troopers that meet the Requirements may turn on the spot, without changing position, to modify their LoF angle as their player chooses. **Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. Smoke Ammunition generates a Zero Visibility Zone (see: Special Terrain, Visibility Conditions, page 143) the size of a Circular Template and with infinite height. Hackers within the Zone of Control of an enemy Repeater may use it to apply Hacking Programs against any enemy Hacker, but applying Firewall MODs (-3). ## 259. What happens under CAUTIOUS MOVEMENT when both “Restriction” and “If the Cautious Movement begins or ends within the LoF or ZoC of any…” apply?

## 276. What happens under CLIMBING PLUS when both “Climbing Plus also allows the user to move on vertical surfaces as if performing…” and “The Climbing Plus Special Skill allows its user to perform other Basic Short Skills…” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. Climbing Plus also allows the user to move on vertical surfaces as if performing normal Movement on a horizontal surface when declaring Move or Dodge, or during movement due to a failed Guts Roll. The General Movement Rules and the Moving and Measuring chapters must be applied. The Climbing Plus Special Skill allows its user to perform other Basic Short Skills or Short Skills (e.g. Move + BS Attack) while moving on a vertical surface, or if hanging from a height (see Trooper Activation, p. 13).

## 277. What happens under DOCTOR when both “The target must have the Vitality Attribute and be in Unconscious State” and “By passing a Normal WIP Roll, the target’s Unconscious State is cancelled, by removing…” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. The target must have the Vitality Attribute and be in Unconscious State. By passing a Normal WIP Roll, the target’s Unconscious State is cancelled, by removing 1 single Wound from those it has received, unless a rule or Skill specified otherwise. If the WIP Roll fails, the target automatically enters the Dead State and is removed from the game table.

**Sources:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 90 — DOCTOR

The target of the Intuitive Attack (or Main Target if using a Template Weapon) must be outside the user’s LoF due to a Zero Visibility Zone, or be in a State that would normally prevent Attacks from being performed against them without previously being Discovered, such as the Camouflaged State. That Trooper must be on the battlefield in the form of a Model or Marker and have either a Cube or the REM Troop Type. If the user has the Lieutenant Special Skill this is also transferred, including any additional abilities and MODs such as (+1 Order). **Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. While off-table, the Trooper will not add their Order to the Order Pool during the Tactical Phase. However, they may enter the table using their own Order to use this Special Skill, when the player chooses. The player may deploy the Trooper on any surface of the game table, as long as that surface’s size is equal to or larger than their base and is in contact with the edge of the table.

## 311. What happens under SUPPRESSIVE FIRE when both “Enemy Troopers within Suppressive Fire Range (0-24 inches) apply a -3 MOD to their…” and “Suppressive Fire allows the affected Trooper to react in ARO with the full B3…” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. Enemy Troopers within Suppressive Fire Range (0-24 inches) apply a -3 MOD to their Attribute in all Face to Face Rolls against a Trooper in Suppressive Fire State. Suppressive Fire allows the affected Trooper to react in ARO with the full B3 value of the SF Mode. The full Burst must always be directed against a single target and cannot be divided between several active enemies (when reacting against a Coordinated Order, for example).

**Sources:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 171 — SUPPRESSIVE FIRE

That Trooper must be on the battlefield in the form of a Model or Marker and have either a Cube or the REM Troop Type. At the end of the Order in which the user entered Unconscious State, before making Guts Rolls, the Trooper makes a Direct Template Attack, placing the center of the Circular Template (Blast Focus) over the center of their base. ## 410. What happens under REQUEST A SPECBALL when both “When Requesting a Speedball during their Active Turn” and “Before making the Combat Jump Roll, the player needs to choose what support equipment…” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. When Requesting a Speedball during their Active Turn, players must place one 55 mm SpecBall Token on the game table applying the Combat Jump Skill rules, and then make a PH Roll for it with a PH value of 15. This cannot be done partway through the Order Expenditure Sequence. Before making the Combat Jump Roll, the player needs to choose what support equipment this suborbital drop contains, choosing two different items from the SpecBall Chart.

## 412. What happens under SUPPRESSIVE FIRE when both “While in Suppressive Fire State, the SF Mode profile replaces the selected weapon’s usual…” and “Suppressive Fire allows the affected Trooper to react in ARO with the full B3…” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. While in Suppressive Fire State, the SF Mode profile replaces the selected weapon’s usual BS Weapon profile. The SF Mode profile is the one that will be used while in Suppressive Fire State. Suppressive Fire allows the affected Trooper to react in ARO with the full B3 value of the SF Mode. The full Burst must always be directed against a single target and cannot be divided between several active enemies (when reacting against a Coordinated Order, for example).

## 416. What happens under DOCTOR when both “By passing a Normal WIP Roll, the target’s Unconscious State is cancelled, by removing…” and “may be recovered from the Unconscious State as many times as necessary, as long…” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. By passing a Normal WIP Roll, the target’s Unconscious State is cancelled, by removing 1 single Wound from those it has received, unless a rule or Skill specified otherwise. If the WIP Roll fails, the target automatically enters the Dead State and is removed from the game table. Troopers may be recovered from the Unconscious State as many times as necessary, as long as the Doctor passes the WIP Roll.

**Sources:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 90 — DOCTOR

## 439. What happens under COORDINATED ORDER when both “When players declare a Coordinated Order” and “In a Coordinated Order, the Spearhead Trooper uses half the indicated Burst (B) of…” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. When players declare a Coordinated Order, they must name one of the participating Troopers as that Order’s Spearhead and place a Spearhead Token (SPEARHEAD) beside them. In a Coordinated Order, the Spearhead Trooper uses half the indicated Burst (B) of their weapon, Equipment or Special Skill, including any bonuses, rounded up.

**Sources:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 129 — COORDINATED ORDER

## 441. What happens under EXPLODE when both “At the end of the Order in which the user entered Unconscious State, before…” and “Enemy Troopers who are affected by the Attack and had performed a Dodge will…” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. At the end of the Order in which the user entered Unconscious State, before making Guts Rolls, the Trooper makes a Direct Template Attack, placing the center of the Circular Template (Blast Focus) over the center of their base. Enemy Troopers who are affected by the Attack and had performed a Dodge will avoid this Attack if they passed their PH Roll.

**Sources:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 92 — EXPLODE

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. Troopers equipped with this piece of Equipment do not make Face to Face Rolls against weapons that use Smoke Ammunition. Users of a Multispectral Visor L3 ignore Surprise Attack MODs if they have LoF to the attacker. In addition, they also ignore Surprise Attack MODs from CC Attacks even if they don’t have LoF to the attacker.

## 443. What happens under REQUEST A SPECBALL when both “When Requesting a Speedball during their Active Turn” and “Restriction: SpecBall items are unique and non-transferable” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. When Requesting a Speedball during their Active Turn, players must place one 55 mm SpecBall Token on the game table applying the Combat Jump Skill rules, and then make a PH Roll for it with a PH value of 15. This cannot be done partway through the Order Expenditure Sequence. Restriction: SpecBall items are unique and non-transferable.

**Sources:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 98 — REQUEST A SPECBALL

## 444. What happens under SUPPRESSIVE FIRE when both “While in Suppressive Fire State, the SF Mode profile replaces the selected weapon’s usual…” and “The SF Mode profile only alters the Range and B values of the weapon…” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. While in Suppressive Fire State, the SF Mode profile replaces the selected weapon’s usual BS Weapon profile. The SF Mode profile is the one that will be used while in Suppressive Fire State. The SF Mode profile only alters the Range and B values of the weapon being used. The weapon’s PS, Ammunition, etc. remain unchanged.

## 448. What happens under DOCTOR when both “By passing a Normal WIP Roll, the target’s Unconscious State is cancelled, by removing…” and “A Trooper with the Doctor Special Skill can use it on themselves, to cancel…” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. By passing a Normal WIP Roll, the target’s Unconscious State is cancelled, by removing 1 single Wound from those it has received, unless a rule or Skill specified otherwise. If the WIP Roll fails, the target automatically enters the Dead State and is removed from the game table. A Trooper with the Doctor Special Skill can use it on themselves, to cancel any states that can be canceled by Doctor. However, this cannot be done if the Trooper is in a Null State.

**Sources:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 90 — DOCTOR

If the user passes a Normal WIP Roll, applying the same MODs as in a BS Attack (Cover, Range, Mimetism…), the target Marker is removed, and must be replaced with the actual Trooper Model, or Weapon or Equipment Token. A Trooper that fails a WIP Roll to Discover a Marker cannot attempt to Discover the same Marker until the next Player Turn.

## 460. What happens under DOCTOR when both “By passing a Normal WIP Roll, the target’s Unconscious State is cancelled, by removing…” and “Doctor (2W): Allows the target to remove 2 Wounds when their Unconscious State is…” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. By passing a Normal WIP Roll, the target’s Unconscious State is cancelled, by removing 1 single Wound from those it has received, unless a rule or Skill specified otherwise. If the WIP Roll fails, the target automatically enters the Dead State and is removed from the game table. Doctor (2W): Allows the target to remove 2 Wounds when their Unconscious State is cancelled.

## 468. What happens under SUPPRESSIVE FIRE when both “Suppressive Fire allows the affected Trooper to react in ARO with the full B3…” and “The SF Mode profile only alters the Range and B values of the weapon…” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. Suppressive Fire allows the affected Trooper to react in ARO with the full B3 value of the SF Mode. The full Burst must always be directed against a single target and cannot be divided between several active enemies (when reacting against a Coordinated Order, for example). The SF Mode profile only alters the Range and B values of the weapon being used. The weapon’s PS, Ammunition, etc. remain unchanged.

**Sources:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 171 — SUPPRESSIVE FIRE

If the user passes a BS Attack Roll, applying the corresponding MODs (for Range, Cover, etc.), the target performs a single PH Roll. A MediKit can also be used in Silhouette contact. ## 480. What happens under SUPPRESSIVE FIRE when both “Suppressive Fire allows the affected Trooper to react in ARO with the full B3…” and “This state does not interfere with Automatic Special Skills or Automatic Equipment, which will…” apply?

**Answer — EXPLICIT RULES ANSWER:** Apply both explicit rules. Suppressive Fire allows the affected Trooper to react in ARO with the full B3 value of the SF Mode. This state does not interfere with Automatic Special Skills or Automatic Equipment, which will remain functioning normally.

## 498. Can a Trooper use Cautious Movement while inside an enemy Hacker’s Hacking Area without generating an ARO?

**Answer — EVIDENCE-BOUNDED INTERPRETATION:** A non-Hackable Trooper does not generate a Hacking ARO merely for beginning or ending Cautious Movement inside an enemy Hacking Area. A Hackable Trooper does generate AROs as usual in that situation.

**Sources:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 32 — CAUTIOUS MOVEMENT; Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 55 — HACKING AREA

## 499. Does a TinBot continue providing its bonus after its owner enters Dead State?

**Answer — EVIDENCE-BOUNDED INTERPRETATION:** No. The TinBot is removed when its owner enters Dead State, so its MODs or advantages no longer apply.

**Sources:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 127 — TINBOT; Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 159 — DEAD

## 500. Can an enemy claim Partial Cover from another player’s Deployable Cover?

**Answer — EVIDENCE-BOUNDED INTERPRETATION:** No. The Deployable Cover rule expressly prevents enemy Troopers from claiming Partial Cover from that Deployable Cover, even though it remains a Scenery Element for movement and positioning.

**Sources:** Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 122 — DEPLOYABLE COVER; Infinity Rules N5.3, p. 41 — PARTIAL COVER
